### 2026-09-09 13:46:54  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Snaps 2-Session Losing Streak, Ends 0.2% Higher” — New Zealand stocks climbed 27 points, or 0.2%, to close at 13,819 on Wednesday, halting losses from the previous two sessions, buoyed by gains in financials, utilities, and communication services. Tuesday’s report from Moody’s ratings agency continued to support sentiment, saying New Zealand’s economic recovery is underway, though warning that several issues remain unresolved. However, a downbeat session on Wall Street overnight limited the gains, as rising oil prices amid the escalating conflict in the Middle East raised concerns over inflation and boosted bets that central banks would raise interest rates. Fresh data from China, New Zealand’s top trading partner, also capped the index, as consumer and producer price pressures continued to rise amid higher oil prices. Among the top movers were Sanford (1.6%), Mercury NZ (1.4%), Contact Energy (1.2%), Mainfreight (0.9%), Infratil (0.8%), ANZ Group (0.7%), Delegat Group (0.7%), and A2 Milk (0.5%).

### 2026-09-09 13:43:26  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #NVDA.O #Impact bullish

【Morgan Stanley: AI capital ROIC could reach 25%–50% if GPUs sustain ~70% utilization】Morgan Stanley estimates incremental ROIC for three GPU monetization models at roughly 31% for GPU leasing, 46% for running model APIs on owned compute, and 25% for renting third‑party compute. These figures refer to incremental returns on a 1 GW GB300 capacity once in stable operation, not company‑level returns. Returns depend on GPU utilization, pricing and per‑unit build cost. Base case assumes ~410k GPUs, 75% utilization and $8.5/hr rent. If rental rates run $7–$10/hr, GPU‑leasing ROIC would vary about 23%–39%.

### 2026-09-09 13:38:05  #MKT News #Important #Star Stocks #Apple #Market Themes #Magnificent 7 #AAPL.O #Impact mixed

【Apple event preview: first foldable iPhone starts at $2,000; iPhone 18 Pro line gains upgrades】New Apple CEO John Ternus will make his public debut at the product event, his first opportunity to present strategic direction. Reported pricing: Apple plans to raise iPhone 18 Pro and 18 Pro Max prices by about $100 each, after taking measures to offset higher chip costs. Apple’s first foldable, reportedly named iPhone Duo, would start at $2,000 for a 256GB base model with higher-capacity variants up to roughly $3,000; availability as early as October and offered in white and deep blue. iPhone 18 Pro line will largely retain iPhone 17 Pro styling but adds new lenses and pro-grade imaging software, a smaller Dynamic Island, A20 Pro chipset, a redesigned vapor chamber for better thermal control, and a mechanical aperture on larger models to improve low-light and depth control. Apple Watch Series 12 and Ultra 4 will get new colorways; Series 12 will reintroduce a ceramic case and Ultra 4 will offer longer battery life. Apple plans an entry-level AirPods 5 with substantially upgraded active noise cancellation. Additional rumored launches include a Home hub for smart-home control, a faster-processor Apple TV and new HomePod mini, plus expected formal release dates for iOS 27, iPadOS 27, macOS Golden Gate, tvOS 27, watchOS 27 and visionOS 27. Items reportedly delayed or not launching at this event: standard iPhone 18 pushed to next spring; in-ear AirPods deferred to next year; MacBook Pro with M6 and a new iMac possible before year-end; OLED-touch MacBook Pro late this year or early next; OLED iPad mini expected by end-October.

### 2026-09-09 13:38:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Estonia #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Estonia Trade Deficit Narrows Slightly in July — Estonia’s trade deficit narrowed slightly to EUR 430 million in July 2026 from EUR 444 million in July 2025. Year-on-year, exports climbed 3% to EUR 1,457 million, driven by higher shipments to non-EU countries (+13%), while exports to EU countries were unchanged. Export growth was led by optical, measuring, and precision instruments (+15%), machinery and mechanical appliances (+13%), raw materials and products of the chemical industry (+13%), and miscellaneous manufactured articles (+11%). Meanwhile, imports increased 2% to EUR 1,886 million, supported by higher purchases from EU countries (+3%) and non-EU countries (+4%). Import growth was driven by wood and articles of wood (+17%), optical, measuring, and precision instruments (+13%), base metals and articles of base metals (+12%), and raw materials and products of the chemical industry (+10%). In January-July, the country recorded a trade deficit of EUR 2,324 million, compared with EUR 2,351 million in the same period of 2025.

【最新PISA測評結果公佈 中國內地學生表現居前列】經濟合作與發展組織（經合組織）當地時間8日釋出2025年國際學生評估專案（PISA）結果。中國北京、上海、江蘇和浙江四省市與新加坡學生在科學、數學和閱讀三大領域的綜合表現處於所有參測國家和經濟體最高水平。此次評估共有來自91個國家和經濟體的超過76萬名15歲學生參加，代表全球約3300萬名同齡學生。報告顯示，在首次引入的計算思維問題解決能力評估中，中國澳門學生平均得分572分，新加坡為563分，北京、上海、江蘇和浙江為560分，均高於經合組織國家500分的平均水平。

【Iraq seeks large boost to production quota as OPEC+ assesses capacity】Iraq is seeking a substantial increase in its OPEC+ production quota during the group's capacity review, targeting a 6.0 mln bpd baseline — a marked rise from its recent output levels, according to people familiar with the matter. Iraq hinted in June it could quit OPEC if it is not granted a sufficiently high baseline; such an exit would be a major blow to the group after the UAE’s departure. OPEC+ has commissioned external advisers to assess each member's maximum achievable capacity; the review is due to be completed at the end of this month and is expected to be approved at the Nov. oil ministers' meeting. Venezuela is also closely watching the situation and is considering an exit as it negotiates a major deal with Trump that would give the US substantial control over part of its oil reserves.

### 2026-09-09 13:13:27  #MKT News #Important #Commodities #Precious Metals

【WPIC says platinum market to flip to surplus this year】The World Platinum Investment Council (WPIC) said the platinum market, after three consecutive years of deficits, is expected to move into surplus this year as large-scale investor selling drains demand. Platinum spiked to near $3,000/oz in January during a metals investment rush, then reversed, dropping more than one-third from the peak. WPIC now forecasts an 8.2t surplus in 2026 versus an earlier forecast of a 9.2t deficit; the revision is almost entirely due to reduced investment demand, with ETF holdings expected to fall about 12t this year. WPIC head of research Ed Steck said a rebound in investor demand could create a supply shortfall in H2, but it would not fully offset the large ETF outflows earlier in the year.

### 2026-09-09 12:54:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Thailand #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Bank of Thailand Keeps Door Open to Further Rate Cuts — Bank of Thailand sees a continued need for accommodative monetary policy as the economic recovery remains uneven, the August meeting minutes showed. The Monetary Policy Committee unanimously kept the one-day repurchase rate at 1% for a third consecutive meeting, citing weak and uneven growth. However, policymakers cautioned that further easing outside a crisis could have limited benefits relative to potential side effects. “Although policy space remained, further monetary policy easing in a non-crisis environment might yield limited additional benefits relative to its potential side effects,” the minutes said. With the policy rate already low by historical standards, the committee stressed the need to preserve room to respond to unexpected shocks. It also flagged risks from the Middle East conflict, trade protectionism and inflation.

### 2026-09-09 12:51:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Netherlands #Personal Spending #Importance 1

Dutch Household Spending Growth Slows in July — Household consumption in the Netherlands rose by 1.2% year-on-year in July 2026, the softest growth in three months, slowing from a 1.7% increase in June. The slowdown was mainly driven by weaker growth in spending on goods, with consumer goods purchases rising 2.2%, down from 3.2% in June. Durable goods recorded the strongest growth, but at a softer pace of 3.1% (vs 5.5%), as households bought more cars, clothing, electrical appliances, shoes, and household goods. Spending on food, beverages and tobacco also grew at a slower pace of 2.0% (vs 2.8%), while consumption of other goods moderated too (0.4% vs 1.0%). Household consumption services also softened (0.5% vs 0.8%), despite higher spending on transport and communication, medical services, recreation and culture, and housing. Spending on services accounts for well over half of total domestic household consumption expenditure.

Rupiah Strengthens Further Despite Oil Price Risks — The Indonesian rupiah firmed below IDR 17,600 per U.S. dollar on Wednesday, extending its recent gains amid optimism over Indonesia’s external position. Forex reserves rose to a five-month high in August, supported by government external-loan withdrawals, reinforcing expectations that buffers will remain ample in the coming months. Domestic sentiment also improved, with consumer sentiment hitting a three-month high in August on stronger household finances and brighter economic outlooks. Meanwhile, authorities intensified market interventions to curb food price volatility after annual inflation quickened to 3.19% in August. However, strength was capped by concerns over oil prices near USD 100 per barrel straining the state budget. Meanwhile, traders cautiously awaited July retail sales data following a third monthly decline in June. Globally, the dollar index steadied after recent losses as elevated oil prices rekindled inflation worries and tempered bets for aggressive Fed easing.

Among stocks in focus, Coforge tumbled more than 5% after Chairman Om Prakash Bhatt resigned following concerns raised by an internal audit over the company’s board evaluation process. ### 2026-09-09 12:25:23  #MKT News

【OpenAI did not start from scratch: compute completed the last mile, original research routes remain scarce】Quanta reports OpenAI’s recent math advance built on prior academic work rather than arising from a blank slate. Mathematicians Diego Córdoba and Luis Martínez‑Zoroa had earlier proposed an “infinite cascade” approach and in 2023 proved singularities under relatively coarse forcing; the remaining technical hurdle was maintaining mathematical smoothness of the forcing. Princeton mathematician Charles Fefferman acknowledged the new result but identified Córdoba and Martínez‑Zoroa as the key contributors. Observers characterise the development as AI industrialising the research “last mile”: humans supply directions, many agents run parallel trial-and-error, and models fill in and formalise critical proofs. Terence Tao warns this could shift mathematics from “proof scarcity” to “proof abundance,” with future scarcities being high‑value questions, viable solution paths and independent verification capacity. The piece concludes compute is accelerating research pace but does not yet imply fully autonomous original research capability.

### 2026-09-09 12:08:19  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Malaysia Industrial Output Growth at 4-Month Low — Industrial production in Malaysia increased by 4.7% year-on-year in July 2026, easing from a 6.5% rise in the previous month and below market forecasts of 5.4%. It was the softest increase in industrial production since March, due mainly to softer rises in manufacturing (6.4% vs 7.3%), driven by lower production of transport equipment and other manufactures (4.5% vs 9.4%), petroleum, chemical, rubber & plastic (1.7% vs 2.6%), and electrical and electronics products (13.3% vs 13.6%). Meanwhile, electricity output also eased to 5.0% from 6.7%. Additionally, mining fell (-3.2% vs 3.1%), as crude oil and condensate output declined at a faster pace (-13.1% vs -3.3%), while gas production eased sharply (3.6% vs 7.4%). For the first seven months of the year, industrial production grew 5.7% yoy, driven by manufacturing (6.5%), electricity (6.1%), and mining (2.0%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, industrial output edged down 0.1% in July, following a 0.5% fall in the preceding period.

### 2026-09-09 12:00:34  #其他

【我國進一步規範醫療機構麻醉藥品和精神藥品管理】為進一步健全醫療機構麻精藥品管理機制，保障臨床合理需求，防範流入非法渠道，國家衛生健康委會同國家中醫藥局、國家疾控局、中央軍委後勤保障部在2005年釋出實施的《醫療機構麻醉藥品、第一類精神藥品管理規定》基礎上，修訂形成了《醫療機構麻醉藥品和精神藥品管理規定》，將於2026年10月1日起實施。

《管理規定》共11章73條，聚焦麻精藥品採購、儲存、開具、使用、回收、銷燬等關鍵環節，細化完善了各項管理要求。

一是將適用範圍由藥用類麻醉藥品、第一類精神藥品擴大至全部藥用類麻精藥品（包括藥用類麻醉藥品、第一類精神藥品和第二類精神藥品）。
二是細化了醫療機構內部管理體系和培訓考核要求，完善了藥房、麻醉科、手術室等重點科室管理，以及回收銷燬、安全應急等工作機制。
三是增加了知情同意相關規定。要求醫師在為擬帶離醫療機構使用麻精藥品的患者開具處方時，充分告知麻精藥品可能涉及毒品犯罪的法律屬性。為未成年人開具麻精藥品處方，需取得其監護人書面知情同意。
四是積極發揮資訊化在醫療機構麻精藥品管理中的作用。促進依託數智技術加強醫療機構麻精藥品全流程管理，提升管理效能。

### 2026-09-09 11:51:19  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #Market Regions #Japan & APAC #USDJPY #Impact bullish #DXY #US10Y #Impact bearish

【BlackRock: Japan rate reset spilling across borders】BlackRock strategists say intensified capital competition means Japan’s rate reset is spilling across borders. They outline a bond-market feedback loop: rising U.S. rates could weaken the yen and pressure the Bank of Japan to act sooner; higher Japanese yields could pull capital home, reducing demand for U.S. Treasuries and raising U.S. borrowing costs. Decades of ultra-low domestic yields made Japan a major capital exporter; Japanese holders now own about $1.1trn of U.S. Treasuries. A 5% repatriation would be roughly $55bn — about one-quarter of last year’s foreign net purchases of U.S. Treasuries.

### 2026-09-09 11:48:51  #兩會

【香港特區首任行政長官董建華逝世，李家超表示沉痛哀悼】據香港特區政府新聞公報，香港特區行政長官李家超今日（9月9日）就國家政協原副主席、香港特別行政區（香港特區）首任行政長官董建華逝世表示沉痛哀悼。董建華於1997年擔任香港特區首任行政長官，出任該職位至2005年3月。他於2005年至2023年擔任第十至十三屆全國政協副主席。他在2006年獲頒大紫荊勳章。李家超說：“董建華先生是香港特區首任行政長官。在他的卓越領導下，為香港順利迴歸祖國，貫徹落實‘一國兩制’方針和《基本法》做出了卓越貢獻。在任七年多期間，董建華先生帶領香港成功抵禦亞洲金融風暴的衝擊，並帶領港人一同努力面對各方面的挑戰，讓香港經濟強勁復甦，社會保持繁榮穩定。他更以前瞻視野建立機制，促進香港與內地經貿聯絡與協同發展，為香港長遠競爭力奠下重要基礎，對香港貢獻尤多。”“董建華先生於二○○五年離任行政長官一職後，擔任全國政協副主席，繼續在國家層面建言獻策，繼續為香港的繁榮安定和持續發展作出貢獻。他亦積極組織民間力量，推動香港與國際社會的深度交流，提升香港在國際舞臺上的角色和影響力。董建華先生一生以‘報效國家，服務香港’為己任，我對董建華先生的辭世深感哀痛，他畢生為香港所作的巨大貢獻將永存我們心中。我謹代表香港特區，向他的家人致以深切慰問。”

【海底撈股價大跌 訊息稱張勇妻子擬減持2.59億股】今日，海底撈股價大跌，早盤一度跌超10%。訊息面上，據外媒報道，海底撈大股東計劃在9月9日出售減持2.59億股，配售由瑞銀負責，每股售價約10.6至10.7港元，較上日收市價折讓5.98%至6.85%。摩根士丹利研報稱，減持的大股東為海底撈創始人、董事長張勇妻子舒萍。此次減持令人驚訝，因張勇剛在5月以13.39港元增持1135萬股，並稱公司將此次行動視為股東個人的財務安排。摩根士丹利指出，減持將在短期內對市場情緒造成壓力，直至市場對公司增長重拾信心，同時需要確信股東不會再進行減持，維持對公司“超配”評級，重申目標價16.50港元。截至發稿，海底撈官方暫無回應。（新浪科技）

### 2026-09-09 11:44:12  #MKT News #Market Regions #Wall Street (US) #Macro & Rates #Treasury Yields #US10Y #Impact bullish

【Institutions: market eyes size of US Treasury buyback as Wall Street weighs risks】US Treasury Secretary Bessent will disclose the size of an expanded federal debt buyback program; market participants view the number as a lever to curb rising long-term borrowing costs. Financial institutions currently expect a $5–6 bln operation; economists warn a $4 bln package could disappoint and rekindle selling, while a materially larger size would signal the government is more concerned about market instability. Bessent has defended the interventions, calling the recent sell-off a speculative spike warranting policy response and saying his aim is to restore market equilibrium, not to set long-term rates. Ten-year Treasury yields remain near 2023 highs — a direct driver of US mortgage rates and corporate borrowing costs — leaving the announced buyback size an immediate market-moving variable.

【韓國央行前行長李昌鏞：無需對韓元短期波動過度擔憂】韓國央行前行長李昌鏞表示，民眾無需過分擔憂韓元短期波動，韓國經濟已發生結構性變化，使韓元比以往更具韌性。李昌鏞週三接受採訪時表示，“我們在國際市場上不再是淨債務國，而是淨債權國，所以情況發生了變化，當匯率波動到一定水平時，我們無需恐慌”。當被問及近期美日聯手支撐日元一事時，李昌鏞表示，他對歐洲國家被排除在討論之外感到驚訝，並呼籲加強政策協調。“因為國際資本市場規模龐大，我認為即便是大國也無法獨自應對這些問題，”他說道。他指出，韓國當局必須採取不同的策略。“就我們這樣的小經濟體而言，我認為政策制定者必須保持謙遜，”李昌鏞稱。“我能做的只是平抑市場波動，但我無法左右匯率走勢。只有政策的根本性調整才能做到這一點。”

### 2026-09-09 11:39:58  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Palm Oil Weighed by Weak Exports, India Bottleneck — Malaysian palm oil futures remained subdued, trading near MYR 4,960 per tonne, pressured by a stronger ringgit and weaker edible oil prices in the Dalian and Chicago markets. Meanwhile, exports remained sluggish, with cargo surveyors estimating August palm oil shipments fell between 6.5% and 14.9% from July, while inventories climbed to a five-month high in July. In India, heavy vegetable-oil buying congested major ports, delaying vessel unloading by up to 10 days as storage tanks overflow, potentially dampening near-term import demand. In top supplier Indonesia, new technical regulations tightened central government control over key export commodities, including palm oil, under President Prabowo Subianto’s plan to boost state earnings from natural resources. Still, losses were cushioned by firmer crude oil amid renewed Middle East tensions. Meanwhile, unusually dry conditions across Southeast Asia raised concerns over yields after triggering fires and haze in Borneo and Sumatra.

【貝萊德：日本央行加快升息步伐或推高全球債券收益率】貝萊德表示，日本央行若加快升息步伐，可能推動日本投資者將資金匯回國內以追求更高回報，從而推高全球債券收益率。貝萊德研究部門策略師Wei Li等人在報告中寫道，“溢位效應是切實的”，債券市場存在形成反饋迴圈的風險。“日本目前提供了可觀的無風險收益率”，過去數十年，由於國內收益率長期處於超低水平，日本投資者為尋求收入而將資金投向海外，而隨著利率上升，部分資金可能迴流日本市場。與此同時，鑑於通脹持續，日本的經濟形勢需要收緊貨幣政策，但不斷增長的政府支出和超過GDP兩倍以上的債務使得提高利率的成本更高；“過於寬鬆的貨幣政策對日元造成了壓力”。如果日元走弱，日本當局決定為支撐匯率而出售包括美國國債在內的海外資產，美債收益率可能面臨進一步上行壓力。美國利率上升可能削弱日元，並迫使日本央行更快加息；日本利率上升則可能吸引更多資金迴流本土，削弱對美國國債的需求，從而推高美國借貸成本。

### 2026-09-09 11:15:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Bangladesh #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Bangladesh Inflation Rate at 10-Month Low — The annual inflation rate eased to 8.26% in August 2026 from 8.32% in the previous month, marking the lowest reading since October. Price growth slowed for food and non-alcoholic beverages (7.16% vs 7.02% in July), housing and utilities (9.44% vs 9.48%), health (3.48% vs 3.9%), education (7.34% vs 8.73%), and restaurants and hotels (12.49% vs 12.57%). In contrast, inflation accelerated for transport (9.41% vs 9.34%), alcoholic beverages, tobacco and narcotics (11.83% vs 10.58%), clothing and footwear (9.94% vs 9.75%), furnishings, household equipment and routine household maintenance (6.67% vs 6.58%), communication (7.75% vs 6.9%), recreation and culture (9.3% vs 8.8%), and miscellaneous goods and services (14.75% vs 14.49%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 2.31%, accelerating from 1.44% in July and marking the highest reading since August 2025.

### 2026-09-09 11:10:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #Indonesia #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Indonesia Consumer Mood Rises to 3-Month High — Indonesia’s consumer confidence index increased for the first time in four months to 118.5 in August 2026 from 116.8 in the previous month, which was the lowest level since April 2025. It was the highest reading since May, largely boosted by stronger assessments of current economic conditions, which rose by 1.5 points to a three-month high of 109.4. Sentiment toward durable goods purchases also increased by 2.0 points to 106.1, while perceptions of job availability over the past six months advanced by 3.0 points to 104.1. Meanwhile, expectations for income over the next six months went up 1.3 points to 134.9. At the same time, expectations regarding job availability also rose by 2.6 points to 125.7, while expectations for business activity increased by 1.7 points to 122.1.

### 2026-09-09 11:09:11  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Corn Retreats Ahead of USDA Report — Corn futures fell below $5.10 per bushel, pulling back from an over three-year high of $5.21 hit on September 1, as traders adjusted positions ahead of Friday’s widely anticipated USDA supply-and-demand report. The decline came despite expectations for weaker US yields, which have continued to provide a floor for prices. Commodity brokerage StoneX lowered its estimate of the average US 2026 corn yield to 182.9 bushels per acre from 184.8 in its previous monthly forecast, raising concerns over tighter supplies. Meanwhile, warmer and drier weather across the US Midwest in the coming weeks is expected to limit harvest delays and reduce frost risks, potentially allowing fieldwork to progress quickly. The USDA reported that 5% of the US corn crop had been harvested as of last Friday, while 56% was rated good to excellent. Traders are also watching risks to Black Sea grain exports as the ongoing Russia-Ukraine war threatens to disrupt supplies and tighten global grain markets.

New Zealand Dollar Holds Decline — The New Zealand dollar traded around $0.585 after two consecutive sessions of modest losses, as markets reassessed the interest-rate outlook from the Reserve Bank of New Zealand. Markets now imply around an 80% chance of the central bank leaving rates unchanged in October, while a December increase remains largely priced in. The kiwi dollar also struggled at a 13-year low against the Australian dollar, amid diverging monetary-policy paths between the neighboring nations, though both currencies received some support from a weaker US dollar.

### 2026-09-09 10:52:17  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Copper Slips on Profit-Taking — Copper futures eased to around $6.66 per pound on Wednesday, retreating from record levels as traders took profits while reassessing market fundamentals. The metal had surged to fresh all-time highs earlier this week amid tariff uncertainty and tightening global supply. The persistent premium for copper futures on New York’s Comex continued to offer arbitrage opportunities, even as the White House has yet to decide on proposed tariffs for the metal. Meanwhile, major copper-producing nations in South America have encountered operational difficulties this year, weighing on production and exports. Supply has also struggled to keep up with growing demand from data centers, renewable energy projects and electricity grids. In China, demand is expected to strengthen as the market enters the traditional peak season for manufacturing activity.

### 2026-09-09 10:40:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

China 10Y Yield Approaches 2025 Low — China’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 1.67% on Wednesday, nearing its lowest level since mid-June 2025, as banks increasingly turned to government debt to boost returns amid weak demand for mortgages and consumer loans. Claims on the government rose to 16.4% of lenders’ total assets in July from 11.5% five years earlier, while claims on residents fell to 16.4% from 20.3%. The shift marked the first-ever reversal between the two categories, highlighting Beijing’s struggle to revive credit demand following an unprecedented property downturn and a prolonged consumption slump. On the economic front, annual consumer inflation rose to 0.8% in August, matching market expectations and accelerating from a six-month low of 0.5% in July. Producer price inflation also picked up to 3.8% from 3.5%, surpassing expectations of 3.7%. The renewed price pressures were driven largely by higher energy costs amid ongoing tensions in the Middle East.

【日元急升之際日本散戶逆勢加碼做空 淨空頭倉位達3.6萬億日元】日本散戶正押注日元這波急漲難以持續，即使日元已升至數月來最高水準，他們仍持續加碼看空倉位。根據日本金融期貨協會及東京金融交易所的資料，日本個人投資者上週持有的日元淨空頭倉位估計達3.61萬億日元(235億美元)，高於8月。此前日元空頭頭寸在7月一度達到 4.41萬億日元，創2015年以來最高。日本散戶投資者向來偏好逆勢操作，日元走強時賣出，走弱時則買進。這與外國投資者形成鮮明對比；隨著日元大漲，海外投資者紛紛解除以日元融資的套利交易。對沖基金也建立日元進一步升值的倉位，部分基金甚至押注美元兌日元年底前將跌破150。瑞穗銀行高階策略師Masayuki Nakajima談到日本散戶時表示，如果日元持續升值，這些投資者最終可能被迫平倉美元多頭倉位。他表示，屆時投資者為停損而進一步賣出美元，反過來可能加劇日元升勢。

While major central banks face pressure to keep rates elevated or tighten policy further, China's comparatively low borrowing costs allow investors to finance higher-yielding assets at a lower cost. On the economic front, annual consumer inflation rose to 0.8% in August, matching forecasts and accelerating from a six-month low of 0.5% in July. The increase in price pressures was driven mainly by higher energy costs amid ongoing tensions in the Middle East.

China Stocks Gain After Inflation Data — The Shanghai Composite edged up 0.1% to 3,940.4 on Wednesday, while the Shenzhen Component rose 0.5% to 13,767, even as renewed inflationary pressures from higher energy costs added to concerns about China's slowing economy. Annual consumer inflation rose to 0.8% in August, matching forecasts and accelerating from a six-month low of 0.5% in July. Producer price inflation also picked up to 3.8% from a three-month low of 3.5%, exceeding market expectations of 3.7%. The pickup in inflation was driven mainly by higher energy costs amid ongoing Middle East tensions. While the country has emerged from a prolonged period of deflation, weak consumer demand continues to limit companies' ability to pass rising costs on to households as the economy slows and fiscal support becomes more restrained. Notable gainers included Hygon Information Technology (3.9%), Zhongji Innolight (1.7%), Luxshare Precision Industry (1.1%), and Suzhou Dongshan Precision (3.1%).

【第九個中國農民豐收節總體安排公佈 將舉辦47項重點活動】今天上午，農業農村部舉行新聞釋出會，介紹今年豐收節活動總體安排的有關情況。今年農曆秋分是第九個中國農民豐收節，總體安排分為兩個層面。一是組委會牽頭組織開展系列慶豐收活動。中央宣傳部、教育部等17個組委會成員單位共同參與，將組織47項重點活動，可以概括為“1+N”。“1”是全國主場活動，將在湖北省襄陽市棗陽市東郊村舉辦。“N”是以農民為主體，組委會成員單位將組織開展全國大型義診活動周、全國科普月農業農村主場活動、金秋消費季等46項重點活動，涵蓋科技、普法、義診、文體等服務內容。二是各地組織開展豐富多彩的為農服務和慶祝活動。圍繞豐收節主題，聚焦農民群眾生產生活需要，從展示豐收成果、展現農民風采、弘揚農耕文化、促進農產品消費等方面開展一系列富有地域特色的慶祝活動。

European Gas Extends Rally — European natural gas prices climbed above €77/MWh on Wednesday, extending gains for a fourth session to their highest level since December 2022, as continuing strikes in the Middle East heightened concerns over further supply disruptions. The US military said it destroyed five Iranian oil tankers near Kharg Island on Tuesday in response to attacks on its warships, while Tehran retaliated against US targets in Jordan. Iran-backed Houthi militants in Yemen also launched drones and missiles at energy facilities in southern Saudi Arabia. These underscored mounting challenges to restoring normal commercial traffic through the Strait of Hormuz. The sustained disruptions have forced Qatar to suspend shipments and extend force majeure on cargoes to Europe and Asia through autumn. The reduced LNG deliveries have slowed European storage injections, with gas inventories remaining below the seasonal average, leaving the market increasingly vulnerable as the winter heating season approaches.

【特朗普據悉向三位白宮女助手私人贈予大額現金】根據特朗普政府公佈的財務披露檔案，唐納德·特朗普向其行政助理娜塔莉·哈普（Natalie Harp）以及另外兩名年輕、忠誠的白宮僱員各贈予了45,000美元的現金。這三名女性僱員在上週晚些時候公開的申報表中，將這筆款項描述為“節日現金禮金”。根據一份提交給美國國會的年度報告，這些禮金的金額約為她們各自在白宮任職期間15萬美元年薪的三分之一。小布什總統時期的白宮首席道德律師理查德·佩因特表示，這些款項似乎違反了一項聯邦法規，該法規禁止利用外部資金為聯邦僱員的薪資進行補充。佩因特稱，按照慣例，政府道德監管官員會警惕這樣一類情形：曾僱傭聯邦官員的企業試圖額外提供資金，以此減輕該官員在政府任職期間的經濟負擔。他補充道，如果僱主給家中僱員發放節日禮品，該禮品會被視作僱員收入的一部分。依據法律，發放額外收入的一方與收受款項的一方均需承擔法律責任。特朗普政府的司法部可能選擇不予執法，但該案件的訴訟時效為五年。特朗普的任期還有兩年多一點就將結束。

Heating Oil Moves Toward Record High — US heating oil prices rose above $4.63 per gallon, moving back toward their record high as refineries grapple with increasingly constrained refined-product supplies. The Middle East war continued to escalate as Iranian-backed Houthis launched strikes on Saudi energy facilities, including the Jazan oil refinery. This comes on top of ongoing disruptions and reduced traffic through the Strait of Hormuz, while Ukrainian attacks on Russian refineries are further limiting global diesel supplies, adding to the impact of Moscow’s existing export restrictions. Meanwhile, demand could increase as seasonal agricultural activity picks up, while the approaching winter heating season could add to pressure on distillate supplies. US refineries were already operating at 98% of capacity in the week ended August 28, leaving limited room to further increase distillate production. EIA data also showed that US distillate fuel stocks were 14% below the five-year average in late August.

【最高法釋出案例懲治搶單“物理外掛”不正當競爭行為】最高人民法院9月9日釋出9件2026年人民法院反不正當競爭典型案例，其中一起涉及利用搶單“物理外掛”產品破壞平臺公平競爭秩序，對護航平臺經濟健康規範發展具有示範意義。某科技公司等三家公司是某外賣平臺及某眾包平臺的運營主體。2024年起，三家公司陸續收到大量投訴，反映部分騎手使用外掛裝置在某眾包平臺搶單。三家公司經調查發現，某電子公司在某電商平臺開設兩家店鋪，對外銷售名為“點滑器”的產品，併發布該產品的使用教程影片。教程指導騎手將相關裝置與該眾包平臺App進行連線，透過設定指令，實現無需手動觸碰手機螢幕即可快速重新整理訂單、自動搶單，搶單速度遠快於常規手動操作。據統計，該點滑器產品累計銷售額達數百萬元。三家公司認為，某電子公司實施的前述行為構成不正當競爭，遂訴至法院。（新華社）

### 2026-09-09 10:00:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Sri Lanka #Tourist Arrivals #Importance 1

Sri Lanka Tourist Arrivals Fall Again in August — The number of foreign tourist arrivals in Sri Lanka fell by 3.3% year-on-year to 191,704 in August 2026, following a 1.7% decrease in the previous month. This marked the fifth year-on-year monthly decline so far this year, although the cumulative tourist arrivals for the first eight months of 2026 successfully crossed the 1.5 million milestone, reaching 1,535,122. India continued to dominate as the top source market, accounting for 25.0% of arrivals with 47,253 visitors, followed by the UK with 19,588 (10.0%). China rose to third with 12,321 arrivals (6.0%), overtaking Germany, which slipped to fourth with 11,405 (6.0%). France rounded out the top five with 10,885 visitors (6.0%). Meanwhile, Sri Lanka cut its 2026 tourist arrival target to 2.7 million from 3 million and lowered its tourism revenue goal to $4.2 billion from $5 billion amid weakening visitor numbers. The country also lost hosting rights for the February 2027 Women’s Champions Trophy, which was expected to boost tourism.

【中國科學院：低溫鋰離子電容器電解液獲突破 能量密度達傳統電解液8.9倍】鋰離子電容器在低溫高功率儲能場景中具有重要應用潛力。近日，中國科學院電工研究所研究員馬衍偉團隊提出“溶劑化解蔽電解液”設計策略。團隊採用兼具中等極性、低熔點和適度配位能力的γ－戊內酯（GVL）為主體溶劑，二氟草酸硼酸鋰（LiDFOB）為功能鋰鹽，並引入弱配位乙酸乙酯（EA）作為溶劑化調節組分，削弱溶劑對鋰離子的包裹和遮蔽作用，促進陰離子參與構建區域性溶劑化結構，形成弱束縛、陰離子參與的溶劑化解蔽環境。

### 2026-09-09 09:59:56  #MKT News

【Chinese Academy of Sciences develops low‑temperature LIC electrolyte with 8.9x energy density versus conventional carbonate electrolyte】Researchers at the Institute of Electrical Engineering, Chinese Academy of Sciences proposed a solvation de‑shielding electrolyte design for low‑temperature lithium‑ion capacitors (LICs). The formulation uses gamma‑valerolactone (GVL) as primary solvent (medium polarity, low melting point, moderate Li+ coordination), lithium difluoro(oxalato)borate (LiDFOB) as functional lithium salt, and ethyl acetate (EA) as a weakly coordinating cosolvent to weaken solvent encapsulation of Li+ and encourage anion participation in local solvation structure, creating a weakly bound, anion‑participating solvation environment. Full‑carbon LICs with this electrolyte maintained strong energy output at −40°C, delivering electrode‑mass‑based energy density of 106.8 Wh/kg—about 8.9 times that of conventional carbonate‑based electrolytes. The study reports the approach overcomes slow interfacial reaction kinetics at low temperature caused by strong solvation shielding.

### 2026-09-09 09:58:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #China #Food Inflation #Importance 1

China Food Prices Fall for 5th Month — China’s food prices declined 1.4% year-on-year in August 2026, easing from a 1.5% drop in the previous month. While marking the fifth consecutive month of contraction, the latest decline was the mildest in the sequence. Prices fell further for fresh fruit (-0.5% vs -1.1% in July), edible oils (-1.3% vs -1.1%), and dairy products (-1.4% vs -1.5%). Fresh vegetable prices also dropped 2.8%, reversing a 0.3% increase in July. Pork prices, a key component of China’s food basket, continued to decline (-11.8% vs -13.9%), reflecting abundant supply and subdued demand. In contrast, egg prices accelerated (15.0% vs 14.4%).

【Most US workers fear displacement in AI era】A recent Educational Testing Service (ETS) survey found 54% of US workers feel unprepared for new jobs over the next decade versus 49% globally. Seventy-five percent said they lack a clear understanding of what jobs will look like in 2035. Fifty-eight percent said they fear being displaced in the future workforce; the rate rises to 74% for tech workers and 73% for financial‑services staff. ETS identified consistent causes across markets: limited experience with emerging technologies such as AI, automation and data tools, and limited exposure to innovation systems increasingly used in modern work. Some respondents also worried newly acquired skills could become obsolete shortly after learning.

### 2026-09-09 09:55:58  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Aussie Marches Toward 4-Month High — The Australian dollar edged above $0.72, moving toward a four-month high as increasingly hawkish signals from the Reserve Bank continued to reinforce expectations for another rate hike this month. Deputy Governor Andrew Hauser said late Tuesday that the central bank will debate the case for higher rates at its September meeting, citing persistently high inflation and upside risks. Assistant Governor Sarah Hunter also said the RBA has little tolerance for stronger inflation and may need to raise rates for a fourth time this year. The central bank has already increased its cash rate three times in 2026 as surging oil prices linked to the Gulf conflict pushed inflation higher. Markets are now pricing a 74% probability of a 25-basis-point hike at the September 29 meeting, up sharply from less than 10% a month ago, while a November move is fully priced in. Elsewhere, stronger trade data from China also supported the Chinese-proxy Aussie, with China being Australia’s largest trading partner.

Dollar Steadies as Oil Prices Climb — The dollar index steadied around 98.8 on Wednesday, pausing its recent decline as rising oil prices intensified inflation concerns and strengthened expectations for a Federal Reserve rate hike. Oil prices extended their gains after US forces destroyed five Iranian crude tankers near Kharg Island in retaliation for attempted missile attacks on a US warship. Investors are also awaiting key US inflation data this week, which could provide further clues on the Federal Reserve’s policy outlook ahead of next week’s meeting. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 60% chance of a 25-basis-point Fed rate hike following stronger-than-expected jobs data released Friday. The European Central Bank and Bank of Japan are likewise expected to tighten monetary policy this month. Meanwhile, the dollar remained pressured against the yen after Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent warned traders against betting on further weakness in the Japanese currency.

【NBS: China August PPI +3.8% YoY】China's producer price index rose 3.8% YoY in August, up 0.3 percentage points from July, the National Bureau of Statistics said. Biggest upward contributors were coal mining and washing (+26.6%), non‑ferrous metal smelting and rolling (+20.8%), oil and gas extraction (+10.5%), petroleum, coal and other fuel processing (+11.1%), manufacture of chemical raw materials and chemical products (+9.1%), electrical machinery and equipment manufacturing (+5.9%) and computer, communication and other electronic equipment manufacturing (+5.3%); those seven sectors added about 4.24 percentage points to PPI YoY, 0.37ppt more than last month. Largest downward contributors were electricity and heat production and supply, automobile manufacturing, non‑metallic mineral products, pharmaceutical manufacturing, beverage and refined tea manufacturing, and agricultural and sideline food processing, which fell 1.7%–5.3% and together subtracted about 0.74 percentage points from PPI YoY.

### 2026-09-09 09:28:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natural Gas Prices Ease — US natural gas prices fell below $2.90/MMBtu, retreating from eight-week highs, as abundant domestic supplies and solid production weighed on the market. US gas inventories were 5.2% above the five-year seasonal average as of August 28, while output across the Lower 48 rose to 112.9 bcfd so far in September from August’s monthly high of 112.2 bcfd, reinforcing the supply overhang. These supply-side factors offset support from stronger demand. Forecasts for above-average temperatures across the US South through September 17 are expected to keep air-conditioning demand elevated, supporting natural gas consumption in the power sector. At the same time, gas flows to the nine major LNG export plants increased to 18.1 bcfd in September from 17.2 bcfd in August. Demand for US LNG from Europe and Asia has also risen sharply as buyers seek to replace disrupted Middle Eastern supplies and replenish inventories ahead of the winter heating season.

【Japan's big three post double-digit YoY drops in August China sales】As of Sept 9, Toyota, Nissan and Honda reported August China new-vehicle sales, all down double-digit YoY. Toyota sold 118,400 units in August, down 22.8% YoY and the seventh consecutive month below year-ago levels. Nissan sold 28,275 units, down 51.9% YoY and near-halved; this is Nissan's fifth straight month of YoY decline. Honda sold 26,749 units, down 49.9% YoY and near-halved; Honda has been below year-ago levels for 31 consecutive months. Analysts say intensifying price competition among Chinese EV makers is shifting demand toward lower-priced EVs and away from Japanese automakers' internal-combustion models.

### 2026-09-09 09:24:35  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #CNH #Impact mixed

【Liquidity gauge: market rates rebound toward policy rate】Early Sept. 9, DR007 rebounded to 1.3900%, closing in on the 1.4% policy rate. The PBOC conducted no reverse repos today, recording zero injections and zero withdrawals. DR007 is a short-term money-market rate that reflects funding conditions; the spread versus the PBOC 7-day reverse repo (policy rate) signals market tightness—a sizable DR007 premium over the policy rate may indicate cash scarcity and prompt PBOC liquidity action, while a lower DR007 implies ample funding.

【印度國家證券交易所下調IPO價格區間，無緣印度最大上市交易】據知情人士透露，印度國家證券交易所（NSE）已下調籌備已久的首次公開發行（IPO）價格區間，還可能縮減發售股份規模，使其原本有望成為印度史上最大規模上市的計劃落空。不願具名的訊息人士稱，本次IPO發行價區間大機率定為每股1700至1785盧比，低於此前推介的2000至2100盧比區間。訊息人士表示，由於部分股東不願在降價後出售股份，發售股權比例或將由原定6%縮減至總股本約5.5%。按擬定價格區間上限測算，出售5.5%股權可募資約2430億盧比（合26億美元），低於現代汽車印度公司2024年IPO募資的2790億盧比（該募資規模為印度史上最高）。據此計算，NSE估值最高可達4.42萬億盧比（合466億美元），而此前目標估值最高為5.26萬億盧比。

### 2026-09-09 09:13:01  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #SAP.DE #Impact bearish #MSFT.O #ORCL.O

【Bernstein: Incremental ERP spend shifting to AI-native firms】Gartner projects the global ERP market will rise from $65.3bn in 2024 to $126.1bn in 2030, but Bernstein says new spending will concentrate on AI agents. From 2026–2030, simple AI-agent ERP is forecast to grow 66.3% and advanced/autonomous AI-agent ERP 95.0%, while traditional ERP excluding generative AI is expected to shrink 30.1%. Bernstein says AI-native vendors are targeting high-friction workflows—financial close, accounts payable, revenue recognition, purchase requisitions and HR services—without replacing SAP or Oracle. By controlling the UI, workflow orchestration and task execution they can capture the fastest-growing, highest-monetization segments of the ERP market. Bernstein expects incumbents to retain transaction data, permissions, audit and compliance layers while AI-native firms handle top-layer interaction and execution, pointing to a coexistence model. The report flags a long-term risk to SAP, Oracle and Microsoft of ERP moving to the backend and being accessed via external AI agents.

Australian Stocks Subdued Ahead of China Data — Australian shares steadied on Wednesday morning, hovering near 8,910 after Tuesday’s sharp drop to a six-week low. Bargain hunting in non-energy minerals, energy stocks, and tech helped offset weakness in healthcare, commercial services, and financials. Sentiment remained fragile as oil prices surged and global inflation worries deepened, with local business confidence slipping to a three-month low in August and consumer sentiment sliding in September. Meanwhile, U.S. futures weakened after Wall Street’s overnight losses, driven by escalating Middle East tensions following attacks on Saudi energy facilities. On the trade front, China logged robust exports and imports in August, with trade surplus topping USD 800 billion so far this year. Miners led gains, with BHP up 2.2%, while Woodside Energy rose 2.3% and Santos added 1.4%. In contrast, three of the four big banks eased between 0.1% and 1%. Traders now await CPI and PPI data from main trading partner China later today.

Yen Extends Gains Amid Bessent Warning — The Japanese yen appreciated toward 153 per dollar on Wednesday, holding near its strongest level in almost seven months after US Treasury Secretary Scott Bessent warned traders on betting against the currency. The former hedge fund manager said he has “pretty good insight” on what the Bank of Japan will do when he is making a call and intervening with the yen. The BOJ is broadly expected to raise interest rates next week, while the Takaichi administration has adopted a more hawkish stance as policymakers acknowledge the need to limit excessive yen weakness. The currency is also reversing the bearish forces that drove it to a 40-year low in July, including an unwinding of carry trades and expectations of increased capital repatriation. Meanwhile, private-sector data showed Japanese manufacturers’ sentiment improved for a second consecutive month in September, helped by resilient demand for semiconductors and data centers.

Investors also kept a close watch on the yen after the currency strengthened to its highest level in nearly seven months. Indonesia Motorbike Sales Rise 3.18% in August — Motorbike sales in Indonesia increased 3.18% year-on-year to 596,461 units in August 2026, slowing from an 8.3% rise in the previous month. The Association of Indonesian Motorcycle Industry (AISI) expects sales to remain relatively stable at around 6.4 million to 6.7 million units this year. Last year, motorbike sales climbed 1.3% to 6.4 million units, within the target range of 6.4 million to 6.7 million units set by AISI. However, the growth rate was slightly slower than the 1.5% increase recorded in 2024. On a monthly basis, motorbike sales fell 6.2% in August, reversing sharply from a 23.0% surge in July.

【CITIC Securities: Shipbuilding sector in decade-long upcycle】CITIC Securities says the shipbuilding sector is in a decade-long upcycle, driven by fleet replacement and tighter environmental regulations. External shocks have created divergence across vessel segments and uneven rotation. Elevated tanker freight rates have triggered an ordering surge: new tanker orders have risen sharply since November 2025 and full-year tanker orders are expected to remain high. CITIC sees bulk carrier orders potentially becoming the next growth driver. Leading indicators: new-build price index for August was 186.34, YoY +0.04%, MoM +0.46%, up five consecutive months, underscoring a supply-tight, seller’s market and supporting a constructive view on the sector.

Silver Holds Decline — Silver traded around $65.5 an ounce on Wednesday after sliding for two straight sessions, pressured by rising oil prices that heightened inflation concerns and strengthened expectations for interest rate hikes. Oil prices continued to climb after US forces destroyed five Iranian crude tankers near Kharg Island, the country’s main oil export hub, in retaliation for attempted missile attacks on a US warship. Investors also braced for key US inflation readings this week for further signals on the Federal Reserve’s next policy move. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 60% chance of a 25-basis-point rate hike next week. Although precious metals are widely considered a hedge against inflation, higher interest rates can reduce demand for non-yielding assets by increasing the appeal of interest-bearing investments. Meanwhile, silver continued to find support from robust industrial demand, particularly from the solar energy and electrification sectors.

【“投資中國不是可選項，而是必選項” 中外資本藉助投洽會加速雙向奔赴】9月8日至11日，由中國商務部主辦的第二十六屆中國國際投資貿易洽談會（以下簡稱“投洽會”）在廈門舉行。參展的頭部金融機構多數亮相本屆投洽會“投資中國”專區，以國際化、數智化的展陳設計展示專業實力，直觀展現頭部資產管理機構積極擁抱高水平對外開放、立足本土面向全球的行業風貌。多家金融機構還搭建面向公眾的溝通橋樑，積極推廣中國特色金融文化、普及金融知識、倡導理性投資理念，不斷拓寬金融文化與投教工作的覆蓋範圍與傳播影響力。包括嘉實基金、建信基金以及國泰海通在內的多家金融機構現場工作人員表示，僅開幕半天，就已有不少國內外投資機構前來接洽，諮詢公司業務情況以及代表性產品線。“外資機構主要諮詢兩點，一是對我們投向A股的基金產品，且對科技板塊的投資機遇更為關注；二是希望國內公募機構更加積極參與海外投資。”廣發基金工作人員稱。在展會現場，多箇中東代表團專程到金融機構展位考察，並圍繞跨境資產配置方案、全球市場投資機遇等話題，與現場工作人員開展深度務實交流。多家金融機構認為，從目前的發展趨勢看，全球資本正持續加碼中國市場。“投資中國不是可選項，而是必選項”已成為跨國公司和全球金融機構的普遍共識。

### 2026-09-09 07:57:25  #公司 #國際

【逛投洽會看前沿科技 機器人組團現身吸引外商】9月8日，第二十六屆中國國際投資貿易洽談會（以下簡稱“投洽會”）在廈門國際博覽中心正式開幕。本次投洽會2號館重點聚焦人工智慧、低空經濟、數實融合三大前沿賽道，一批代表中國前沿科技力量的創新企業集中亮相。從人形機器人到無人機，從竹基複合材料到儲能出海，這場展會正成為觀察中國新質生產力產業走向的一個視窗。在2號館，福建人形機器人創新中心的展臺前圍了不少觀眾。30臺“福智”系列人形機器人正在進行多機協同演示，這是福建省迄今為止規模最大的機器人叢集控制展示。記者在現場瞭解到，該展臺展出的“福智”系列輪臂人形機器人，透過全3D列印輕量化本體設計與自研f-VLA大模型，將整機成本控制在萬元以內，僅為行業同類產品的5%至10%。具身智慧行業發展如火如荼，除了福建人形機器人創新中心外，展會現場還有不少機器人企業亮相，包括智元、加速進化、松霖科技旗下松霖機器人、杭州星慧達機器人、深圳大象安泰等。在廈門松霖機器人展位上，公司現場負責人告訴記者，公司業務以出海為主，主要面向北美、歐洲、亞太等海外市場，投洽會現場吸引了大量國內外潛在合作伙伴的關注，有助於更高效拓展國內外市場。“從今年投洽會現場交流情況看，國際買家的關注點更加偏向場景落地性、實際價值的創造以及可靠性。”

### 2026-09-09 07:55:17  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 2

Gold Holds Losses as Oil Prices Rise — Gold held around $4,350 an ounce on Wednesday after sliding for two straight sessions, pressured by rising oil prices that heightened inflation concerns and strengthened expectations for interest rate hikes. Oil prices continued to climb after US forces destroyed five Iranian crude tankers near Kharg Island, the country’s main oil export hub, in retaliation for attempted missile attacks on a US warship. Investors also braced for key US inflation readings this week for further signals on the Federal Reserve’s next policy move. Markets are currently pricing in roughly a 60% chance of a 25-basis-point rate hike next week. Although gold is widely considered a hedge against inflation, higher interest rates can reduce demand for the non-yielding asset by increasing the appeal of interest-bearing investments. Meanwhile, gold posted strong gains in August, supported by increased investment and hedging demand, along with continued central bank purchases, particularly from China.

【韓國選定220億美元天然氣專案，作為該貿易協定下首個投資專案】韓國已確定將美國得克薩斯州一處燃氣發電專案，作為去年達成的一項貿易協議項下的首個對美投資專案。此前數月，雙方圍繞韓方投資承諾的落實進度產生摩擦，此舉或將有助於緩和這對盟友之間的緊張關係。據韓聯社及其他韓國媒體報道，該專案位於得州恩西納爾，投資額約220億美元，總裝機容量6.3吉瓦。專案將先建成1.4吉瓦燃氣輪機發電機組，後續再新增4.9吉瓦聯合迴圈發電產能，用於滿足人工智慧資料中心激增的電力需求。韓國政府暫未披露專案細節，並於週一表示，在完成包括向韓國國會彙報在內的流程前，不會對外發布任何訊息。媒體報道稱，韓國政府將於9月17日向國會彙報投資談判情況，最早可能在9月18日簽署諒解備忘錄（MOU）。

【Anthropic 研究員因擔憂 AI “失控” 離職】一名 Anthropic 公司研究員出於擔憂，選擇離開人工智慧行業。他認為該實驗室及其競爭對手正競相研發人類無法掌控的 AI 系統，這也表明頭部 AI 企業內部的安全顧慮正不斷加劇。專門透過海量資料訓練新型 AI 模型的研究員雅各布・考克森於週二表示，他將離開這家公司，原因是不想參與全行業爭相研發可自我迭代 AI 系統的競賽。他擔心這類系統可能徹底失控並毀滅人類。這位 27 歲的英國籍研究員曾主修數學。他稱，業內許多同行如今會用 “決戰時刻” 和 “終局” 這類詞彙，來描述走向可自我改進模型的發展趨勢。考克森稱：“按照當前態勢，很多極端推演場景都有可能成真，到明年年底情況或許就會失控。” 他還補充道，各家企業以及中國新興 AI 企業之間相互競爭時，安全層面的取捨權衡不可避免。

【厄爾尼諾擾動 “糖”股期聯袂上漲】9月8日，A股市場涉“糖”概念股漲聲一片。當天中糧糖業、*ST廣糖等糖業股強勢漲停；代糖概念板塊亦大漲，三元生物大漲超14%，華康股份、中糧科技、紅棉股份、保齡寶漲停。期貨市場上，國內白糖期貨主力合約繼續上行，盤中一度創出最近兩個月新高，該合約已連續多個交易日上漲。有專業人士在接受證券時報記者採訪時認為，厄爾尼諾導致的歐盟甜菜減產、印度和泰國的減產預期是本輪行情最核心的驅動力量。“厄爾尼諾導致的歐盟甜菜減產、印度和泰國的減產預期是本輪行情最核心的驅動力量，之後引發的印度進出口政策轉向打破了全球糖貿平衡，將國際糖價漲幅進一步放大。”卓創資訊白糖分析師牛哲表示。同時，隨著供需缺口預期持續發酵，資金集中平倉空頭、轉向多頭配置，放大了短期價格上漲的彈性。光大期貨研究所資源品研究總監張笑金告訴證券時報記者，本輪白糖期價走強的核心因素是原糖期價上漲的帶動以及厄爾尼諾天氣影響未來產量預期。市場對農產品供應的擔憂情緒加劇，且當前糖價處於低位，安全邊際較高，因此該品種受到市場關注。

Oil Hits 3-Month High as US Strikes Iranian Tankers — Crude oil rose above $94 a barrel on Wednesday, scaling three-month highs after the US struck several Iranian tankers near Kharg Island, a major crude export hub. The attacks heightened geopolitical tensions and raised concerns about further disruptions to global oil supplies. A US official said the strikes came in response to an attempted missile attack on a US warship. Tehran retaliated by launching ballistic missiles toward Jordan and warning vessels in the Persian Gulf, urging tanker crews near Kuwaiti and Bahraini ports to “immediately abandon their vessels.” Iran-backed Houthi militants also attacked energy infrastructure in southern Saudi Arabia, including the 400,000-barrel-a-day Jazan refinery. Meanwhile, stronger Chinese oil demand is pushing up prices for African, Canadian and Latin American crude as disruptions in the Strait of Hormuz force refiners to look farther afield for alternative supplies.

### 2026-09-09 07:19:12  #MKT News #ASML.O #Impact bullish #005930.KS

【Samsung, ASML to co-develop next-generation High-NA EUV mask blanks】Samsung Electronics will partner with ASML to co-develop High-NA extreme ultraviolet (EUV) lithography mask blanks, Seoul Economic Daily reports. The collaboration targets an industry shift from 6‑inch to 12‑inch mask blanks used since the 1980s; Samsung aims to be first to establish DRAM mass production on High‑NA EUV and plans to deploy the technology in DRAM from 2028. Samsung also intends to extend High‑NA EUV to 1.4nm advanced logic to support foundry expansion. High‑NA EUV offers a numerical aperture roughly 66% higher than conventional EUV, enabling finer circuit patterning; replacing 6‑inch masks with 12‑inch blanks is intended to raise fab throughput and reduce chip manufacturing costs.

### 2026-09-09 07:12:12  #公司 #市場 #觀點 #國際

【銅價上漲“衝擊波” AI算力引爆全球“搶銅”大戰】9月8日晚間，銅價再度上演狂飆行情。截至記者發稿，倫敦金屬交易所（LME）期銅價格觸及14779美元/噸，再創歷史新高。滬銅主力合約cu2610同步走強，盤中最高觸及每噸111720元，較2025年初不足7萬元/噸的水平上漲超50%。國內銅現貨價格同樣創出新高，達到11.14萬元/噸，單日上漲1370元/噸。資本市場對此已有較為充分的預期。“我們一直判斷銅價大機率要上漲。”一家期貨公司研究員向記者表示。中金公司在2026年下半年展望中明確表示，銅價上漲更具確定性，有望向15000美元/噸新高邁進。產業鏈巨頭同樣偏向看漲。洛陽鉬業管理層在接受記者採訪時表示，銅在供應端仍然偏緊，AI等新興領域帶來新的需求支撐，銅價有望保持穩中向好。紫金礦業判斷，優質銅資源的稀缺性疊加礦端供給約束，已確立銅市長期結構性短缺的格局。銅價為什麼漲？是否還會上漲？高銅價帶來了哪些影響？下游企業如何應對？記者一線調研發現，銅價的這一輪快速上漲，既是新經濟對舊供給的一次猛烈撞擊，也是全球化資源博弈的縮影；既給產業鏈中下游帶來了巨大壓力，也孕育出新的市場機遇。（上海證券報）

【新規落地“倒計時” 基金網路營銷邊界進一步釐清】隨著9月30日節點臨近，《金融產品網路營銷管理辦法》（下稱“辦法”）正式實施進入倒計時。近期，機構監管情況通報根據辦法明確細化要求，金融機構委託第三方網際網路平臺為金融產品網路營銷提供服務，應當建立事前評估機制，持續跟蹤第三方網際網路平臺的合規性、安全性和協議履行情況。業內人士認為，目前基金公司大多已提前整肅與第三方網際網路平臺的合作，通報進一步釐清了基金網路營銷合規邊界。未來，行業有望加速邁向以合規、信任、長期回報為基礎的新生態。（上海證券報）

【採購週期縮短50%以上 人工智慧應用在供應鏈領域加速落地】中國物流與採購聯合會今天釋出《中國供應鏈發展報告（2025-2026）》。根據報告，2025年我國供應鏈領域的人工智慧應用開始加速落地，以生成式大模型為引領，機器視覺、智慧體、具身智慧等多項技術協同演進，已在採購管理、需求預測、庫存最佳化、智慧排程、風險管理等供應鏈管理的核心環節實現深度賦能，顯著提升了供應鏈的響應速度、決策精度與運營韌性。據統計，在採購領域，採用智慧採購供應鏈的企業，相較傳統模式，採購效率可提升30%以上，採購週期可縮短50%以上。在物流供應鏈領域，人工智慧應用滲透率超過37%，其中運輸環節以78%的滲透率居於首位；安全合規和智慧倉儲的滲透率分別為52.73%和47.27%。

Thailand Consumer Morale Highest in 6 Months — The University of the Thai Chamber of Commerce's Consumer Confidence Index climbed to 53.2 in August 2026 from July’s 51.8. It was the third consecutive month of increases and the highest reading since February, helped by the government's co-payment scheme, steady interest rates, and higher prices for key agricultural products, which boosted purchasing power. Thanavath Phonvichai, the university’s president, said planned energy-sector reforms could help sustain growth after the consumer subsidy scheme ends on Sept. 30. The expected passage of the 2027 budget would also lead to public spending supporting the economy after September, he added.

NZX 50 Edges Higher in Morning Trade — New Zealand stocks advanced 17 points, or 0.1%, to 13,810 in Wednesday morning trade, erasing losses from the previous two sessions, supported by gains in communication services, utilities, technology, and real estate. ### 2026-09-09 05:50:56  #MKT News #Market Regions #Wall Street (US) #Macro & Rates #Treasury Yields #US10Y #Impact bullish

【Wall Street eyes Bessent Treasury buyback; analysts say cap could be $10bn】US Treasury Secretary Bessent is set to announce the next round of long-term Treasury buybacks; markets are focused on whether the program will expand and the debt-management signal it conveys. Wrightson ICAP senior economist Crandall says a single buyback of $5–6bn is reasonable though larger operations remain possible. Morgan Stanley analysts put a financing-constrained single-operation cap near $10bn; at that level, quarterly net issuance of >20‑year Treasuries could fall about 55%. Bloomberg strategist Feigen says a larger-than-expected buyback would likely be read as a stronger policy signal and could push long yields lower in the short term, but the absolute size would still be limited versus the overall Treasury market. Barclays strategists Pradhan and Hu expect the Treasury may use open-ended wording such as "at least $4bn each time" to retain flexibility. Market focus will be on announced size, frequency and forward guidance; the 30‑year swap spread may more directly price supply changes.

### 2026-09-09 05:06:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #Chile #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Chile Central Bank Holds Key Rate at 4.5% — Chile’s central bank unanimously kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 4.5% in September, citing heightened uncertainty from the Middle East conflict, rising oil prices and persistent global inflation risks. Oil has approached $100 a barrel, while copper prices climbed above $6.50 a pound. Domestically, economic activity remained weaker than expected in the second quarter and early third quarter, accompanied by slowing domestic demand, job losses and a higher unemployment rate. Headline inflation rose to 4.1% in August, driven by volatile components, while core inflation held at 3.3%. Inflation expectations remain anchored at 3% over two years. The central bank said monetary policy would continue to be assessed meeting by meeting, with risks from the conflict and the possibility of a more prolonged domestic slowdown requiring close monitoring.

### 2026-09-09 05:03:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #El Salvador #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

El Salvador Inflation Picks Up in August — Annual inflation in El Salvador accelerated to 3.19% in August 2026 from 2.49% in July, the highest reading since July 2023. Price growth accelerated for food and non-alcoholic beverages (3.65% vs 2.49%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (2.62% vs 2.11%), health (0.54% vs 0.39%), transport (2.91% vs 2.57%), communications (8.23% vs 4.95%), and miscellaneous goods and services (2.14% vs 1.96%). Clothing and footwear inflation increased to 2.62% from 2.11%. Meanwhile, inflation moderated for education (3.30% vs 3.65%). Deflation was recorded in housing and utilities (-0.32% vs 0.05%), and recreation and culture (-0.91% vs -0.90%). Inflation for furniture and household goods remained unchanged at 1.90%, while restaurant and hotel prices were unchanged at a flat inflation reading.

### 2026-09-09 04:53:30  #市場

【OpenAI內部模型88小時暴力攻克千禧年數學難題】當地時間週二，OpenAI公佈了對千禧年數學難題之一——納維-斯托克斯方程相關問題的解決方案。該方程式用於描述空氣、水和血液等流體的運動。OpenAI還首次透露，該問題解決的背後是一款能力顯著強於GPT-6 Astra的下一代內部模型。圖表顯示，在一組精選開放數學問題上，這款內部模型透過率最高接近48%，約為Astra最高表現的近3倍。OpenAI表示，這項數學突破耗費了數百萬美元的運算成本。研究團隊調動了約1萬個協同AI智慧體，在首批智慧體啟動約88小時後便得出解答。OpenAI研究顯示，三維流體運動可能在有限時間內產生奇點，導致方程對流體作為連續介質的描述出現崩潰。此前，紐約大學數學家Tristan Buckmaster與Anthropic研究員Levent Alpöge剛剛發表了一項針對相關流體動力學方程的AI輔助研究。Buckmaster隨後發表宣告質疑，OpenAI是在聽聞其研究後才著手處理該問題，並採用了他們耗時數月開發的罕見研究方法。OpenAI對此表示，其團隊在對方公開研究前未透過任何方式接觸其工作，也未訪問任何具體使用者資料，並稱雙方的證明方法存在顯著差異。

### 2026-09-09 03:39:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Industrial Production #Importance 1

Argentina Manufacturing Output Falls in July — Argentina’s manufacturing output fell 4.9% year-on-year in July 2026, extending a 2.6% decline in the first seven months of the year. Twelve of the 16 manufacturing divisions posted annual declines. The steepest falls came from other equipment, appliances and instruments (-31.4%), machinery and equipment (-26.7%), clothing, leather and footwear (-15.9%), textiles (-13.0%), non-metallic mineral products (-12.6%), tobacco products (-12.3%), rubber and plastic products (-9.1%), furniture and other manufacturing (-9.1%), metal products (-8.4%), motor vehicles and auto parts (-5.4%), chemicals (-1.3%), and food and beverages (-0.5%). Meanwhile, output increased in petroleum refining (8.1%), wood, paper and printing (7.1%), other transport equipment (2.6%), and basic metals (1.6%). On a seasonally adjusted monthly basis, manufacturing output fell 5.0% in July, while the trend-cycle index declined 0.9%.

【Meta Platforms (META.O) launches personal AI agent Muse; top tier $100/month】Meta Platforms (META.O) launched Muse, a personal AI agent for tasks such as scheduling, form‑filling and home camera monitoring. Muse is powered by Meta’s Muse Spark base models and is offered as a free tier plus $20/month and $100/month subscription plans. Meta AI head Alexander Wang said Muse runs in an isolated environment on Meta’s compute infrastructure, will not read actual passwords or payment data, and will request confirmation before performing sensitive actions; users may opt in to allow Meta to use interaction data for model training after removal of key personal identifiers. Meta said it is exploring commerce and in‑agent transaction monetization as it faces AI safety and privacy scrutiny and pressure on AI returns; CEO Mark Zuckerberg has framed personal AI agents as a future product and revenue priority.

【Danube at Budapest falls to record low of 1 cm】Hungary's water authority monitoring data showed the Danube at Budapest plunged to a new historic low of 1 cm on the 8th (local time). The extreme low has severely disrupted river navigation, with exposed riverbed debris threatening traffic, and Hungary's only nuclear plant, Paks, briefly facing shutdown risk due to cooling-water constraints. Water specialists attribute the fall to extreme drought across the Danube basin; at the end of July the Budapest gauge read 27 cm, already below the prior record of 33 cm set on Oct. 25, 2018.

### 2026-09-09 02:47:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Brazil Yields Drop as Election Race Tightens — Brazil’s 10-year government bond yield fell to arround 14.21% from a near one-month high of 14.90% reached on August 14th, following the release of new polls on the 2026 presidential election. The polls showed President Lula and Senator Flávio Bolsonaro in a technical tie in a potential October runoff. Markets view Bolsonaro as more fiscally restrictive, while elevated domestic yields and weak business activity continue to weigh on the economic outlook. Meanwhile, Brazil’s GDP expanded 0.5% in the second quarter of 2026, slightly above forecasts, driven mainly by agriculture. However, the detailed breakdown was weaker, suggesting economic momentum is fading despite a tight labor market and short-term demand stimulus measures. For the BCB, the data suggest that the effects of monetary tightening are increasingly visible, potentially leaving room for further Selic cuts.

The regulator said it will assess whether X effectively implements age safeguards and whether it provides adequate protection for users under 16 where adult content is allowed; it flagged parental-control features as potentially inadequate, hard to find, and not sufficiently highlighted to new users. European Stocks Close Muted for 4th Session — European stocks closed with small movements for a fourth session as the outlook of higher interest rates by the European Central Bank dented the market's demand for more risk. The Euro STOXX 50 inched higher by 0.1% to 6,413 and the STOXX 600 closed flat at 649.6. Traders added positions that correspond to multiple rate hikes by the ECB this year as natural gas prices benchmark rose to their highest since the Russian invasion of Ukraine, lifting inflation risks and sovereign yields. The central bank is largely expected to deliver a rate hike this week. Industrial stocks closed higher after underperforming in recent sessions, with Rheinmetall, Airbus, and Schneider adding over 2%. On the other hand, insurers remained under pressure with Munich Re and AXA down 2% and 1%, respectively. Luxury brands also dropped after Chinese trade data, with Ferrari and Hermes slipping 2%.

DAX Closes Muted — The DAX 40 pared some early losses to close virtually unchanged at 26,008 on Tuesday, as caution prevailed across European markets. Soaring oil prices and heightened tensions in the Middle East rekindled inflation concerns ahead of monetary policy decisions from major central banks. The ECB is expected to raise borrowing costs by another 25 basis points to 2.5% on Thursday, which would mark the second rate hike this year, while markets anticipate a similar move from the Fed next week. Gains in pharmaceutical stocks, industrials and autos were partly offset by weakness in the technology sector and insurers. Fresenius SE & Co surged 3.2%, leading gains after UBS reiterated its positive outlook on the stock with a Buy rating. Defense stocks such as Rheinmetall also strengthened, rising nearly 3%, supported by favorable analyst commentary. Volkswagen and BMW advanced 2.1% and 1.4%, respectively. On the flipside, Infineon Technologies dropped 3.7% following a downgrade by Morgan Stanley..

French Stocks Edge Higher — The CAC 40 edged 0.1% higher to close at 8,318 on Tuesday, supported by gains in industrial and energy stocks. France’s exports rose 0.3% month over month to €54.7 billion in July, led by natural hydrocarbons, other extractive products and industrial goods. Airbus gained 1%, Thales 0.5%, Air Liquide 1.4%, Schneider Electric 1.4%, Legrand 1.1% and EssilorLuxottica 0.7%. TotalEnergies rose 0.6% as oil prices climbed amid escalating Middle East tensions. Financials, however, declined after eurozone inflation accelerated to 3.3% in August, its highest level in nearly three years and above the ECB’s 2% target, boosting expectations of a 25-bp rate hike. Tanzania Inflation Rate Hits Near 3-1/2-Year High — The annual inflation rate in Tanzania inched up to 4.3% in August 2026 from 4.2% in July, reaching the highest level since April 2023. Upward pressure came primarily from prices of transportation (13.8% vs 13.8% in July); education (3.2% vs 2.8%); restaurants & hotels (3% vs 2.7%); furnishings & household equipment (2.6% vs 2.2%) and housing & utilities (2.5% vs 1.6%). Meanwhile, softer price growth was seen for other CPI items such as food & non-alcoholic beverages (3.7% vs 4.1%) and alcoholic beverages & tobacco (1.5% vs 1.9%). On a monthly basis, the CPI rose by 0.1% in August, after decreasing by a similar margin in July.

### 2026-09-08 23:25:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Arabica Coffee Futures at Over 2-Month Low — Arabica coffee futures extended their decline to around $2.9 per pound, hovering near the lowest since June, as the completion of Brazil’s harvest brings greater availability to the market. The country is heading for a record coffee crop in 2026, benefiting from a strong biennial cycle, expanded production areas and favorable weather. Moreover, Brazilian coffee exports surged 45% over a year ago in August. However, the balance between global supply and demand remains tight and vulnerable to climate risks. ICE arabica coffee inventories continued to fall and reached a 27-year low of 223,712 bags by September 3. Meanwhile, the onset of El Niño in the equatorial Pacific is putting Brazil’s 2027 coffee crop under closer scrutiny. The associated changes in rainfall and temperature patterns in different regions of the country could interfere with crucial stages of the coffee plant's cycle, such as flowering, as well as affecting fruit set, bean filling, and the uniformity of ripening.

【全球搶銅潮提前上演 大量銅流向美國】倫敦金屬交易所三個月銅期貨價格7日創下歷史新高。8日亞洲交易時段，銅價延續上漲態勢，盤中最高升至每噸14617美元。有分析指出，市場普遍押注美國將從2027年1月起對精煉銅加徵15%關稅，推動美國貿易商在新政策生效前提前備貨，大量銅從倫敦金屬交易所位於亞洲和歐洲的倉庫流向美國，一場跨太平洋、跨大西洋的“搶銅潮”提前上演。全球最大銅生產國智利受極端天氣與礦山運營擾動影響，8月銅出口額跌至一年多來新低，與此同時，人工智慧、資料中心建設以及新能源汽車普及，也成為眼下拉動銅需求增長的重要力量。銅回收市場同樣火爆。廢銅收購價同比上漲超20%，市場商戶稱“銅價天天漲、日日新高，根本不愁賣”。電解銅現貨庫存創年內新低，冶煉企業滿負荷運轉。（央視財經）

【New York Fed 1-year inflation expectation dips; unemployment‑rise probability highest since Apr 2020】New York Fed Center for Microeconomic Data August 2026 consumer expectations survey (Aug 3–31): median 1-year inflation expectation fell to 3.58% from 3.63%; short- and long-term inflation expectations unchanged. Median 1-year house‑price growth expectation down 0.2ppt to 3.0%, led by Northeast respondents. One‑year gasoline price expectation rose 1.7ppt to 4.6%; food to 5.3% (+0.3ppt); medical to 9.1% (+0.2ppt); college costs to 6.1% (+0.3ppt); rent to 6.6% (+0.7ppt). Median 1-year income growth expectation edged up 0.1ppt to 2.9%. Average probability that the unemployment rate will be higher in one year rose 1.6ppt to 44.4%, the highest since April 2020.

### 2026-09-08 23:09:05  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Inflation Expectations #Importance 1

US Inflation Expectations Remain Elevated — US consumers’ median one-year inflation expectations were unchanged at 3.6% in August 2026s, according to the Federal Reserve Bank of New York’s Survey of Consumer Expectations. However, expected price growth for gas rose 1.7 percentage points to 4.6%, while food expectations increased 0.3 percentage point to 5.3%. Medical-care price expectations rose 0.2 percentage point to 9.1%. Expectations for college education costs increased 0.3 percentage point to 6.1%, while rent expectations climbed 0.7 percentage point to 6.6%. Meanwhile, the five-year-ahead measure was unchanged at 3%, while three-year-ahead expectations edged down 0.1 percentage point to 3.2%. Elsewhere, median expectations for one-year-ahead earnings growth increased slightly to 2.9%. Concerns about the labour market also intensified, with the mean probability that unemployment will be higher in a year rising 1.6 percentage points to 44.4%, the highest level since April 2020.

Brazil Auto Sales Slip in August — Brazil’s new-vehicle sales fell 1.6% month over month to 275,100 units in August 2026, following a 2.6% increase in July. August had two fewer business days. Light-vehicle sales totaled 263,100 units, with passenger cars up 0.2% to 213,100, while light commercial vehicles fell 5.5% to 50,000. Heavy-vehicle sales totaled 12,000 units, as truck sales slumped 14.1% to 10,000 and bus sales dropped 9.4% to 2,000. Electrified vehicles reached a record 24.4% share, including a record 25,900 fully electric vehicles. Average daily sales were 13,100 units, the second-highest level this year, behind May’s 13,700. Year to date, sales reached 1.975 million units, up 18.4% year-on-year. The 2 million-unit mark was reached in early September, one month earlier than in 2025.

Brazil Vehicle Production Nears 7-Year High — Brazil’s vehicle production increased 6.8% month-on-month to 271.2 thousand units in August 2026, accelerating from a 3.1% increase in July and reaching the highest monthly output since October 2019. Light vehicle production reached 257.5 thousand units, with passenger cars up 9.5% to 214.9 thousand units and light commercial vehicles down 2% to 42.6 thousand units. Heavy vehicle production totaled 13.6 thousand units, with trucks down 2.8% to 11.8 thousand units and buses declining 13.1% to 1.86 thousand units. Production rose 9.4% year-on-year. In the first eight months of 2026, output totaled about 1.9 million units, up 8.5% year-on-year. Despite the strong growth, two-thirds of the additional 150,000 vehicles produced this year were SKD and CKD models, imported in parts and assembled in Brazil.

Gasoline Extends Gains — US gasoline futures traded above $3.29 a gallon, extending gains near a two-week high as refineries face increasing pressure from energy supply disruptions. Attacks were reported at several energy facilities in Saudi Arabia by Iran-backed Houthi militants, while a Russian refinery in the Perm region was also targeted by Ukraine. At the same time, US refineries are operating near full capacity, with some bringing forward seasonal fall maintenance to keep production levels elevated. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories fell by 1.173 million barrels in the week ending August 28th, marking continued drawdowns in recent weeks. As a result, pump prices reached a record Labor Day average of $4.15 a gallon, despite easing demand following the end of the summer driving season, athough further relief could come from the seasonal switch to the cheaper winter-blend. Meanwhile, Iran stated it had made progress with Oman on a temporary safe-passage route through the Strait of Hormuz.

Sugar Futures Consolidate Near 18 Cents — US sugar futures eased toward 18 cents per pound after reaching near 1½-year highs in early September, as the market moved into consolidation.Prices are facing some pressure from India, where measures to increase domestic sugar availability could reduce import requirements and weaken international demand. However, the downside is being limited by expectations of tighter global supplies in the 2026/27 season. The International Sugar Organization forecasts a deficit of around 200,000 tonnes, compared with a surplus of 1.1 million tonnes in 2025/26, while global production is expected to decline 1% to 180.1 million tonnes. Weather risks are also increasing, with a strengthening El Niño potentially bringing drier conditions to parts of South and Southeast Asia. Thailand’s sugar production is expected to fall by at least 17% to below 10 million tonnes, while below-normal monsoon rainfall in India is adding to concerns over Asian output.

### 2026-09-08 22:21:06  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Canada 10-Year Yield Hits Two-Year High — Canada’s 10-year government bond yield rose to 3.80% in September, the highest in over two years, after Canada’s retaliatory tariffs on US goods took effect and raised inflation risks. The counter-tariffs cover $20 billion of US goods, with duties ranging from 15% to 50% on products including steel, furniture, clothing and electronics. US tariffs imposed last month targeted $20 billion, or 5%, of Canadian exports to the US. Higher import prices increased inflationary pressures, while the escalating trade war reduced demand for Canadian bonds as safe-haven assets. Energy-driven inflation also boosted rate hike expectations. The BoC held its key rate at 2.25%, noting that inflation risks had increased while new tariffs had made the growth outlook more uncertain. Governor Macklem said policymakers were prepared to raise rates if inflation remained elevated.

【全球最大主權財富基金擬大幅減持美債】“挪威主權財富基金”是全球規模最大的主權基金，據多家外媒日前披露，該基金的管理機構“挪威銀行投資管理基金”日前應詢致函挪威財政部，就“挪威主權財富基金”下步投資方向提出建議。其中一條重要建議就是將政府債券在該基金所持有的債券投資組合中所佔的比例從目前的70%下調至50%，而美國國債將成為主要減持物件之一。訊息稱，目前“挪威主權財富基金”所持有的美債總額約2150億美元。“挪威銀行投資管理基金”計劃將美債持有量大幅削減近800億美元，美債的配置比例也將從34.1%降至21.9%。“挪威主權財富基金”目前資產管理規模約2.34萬億美元。作為美國國債重要的長期穩定海外投資者，“挪威主權財富基金”計劃大規模減持，所釋放的訊號意義重大。（央視新聞）

【馬斯克稱已實現“零稀土”電機，金力永磁：完全不代表行業的主流方向】作為正式宣佈過為特斯拉提供釹鐵硼磁體的永磁材料供應商，金力永磁在9月8日正面回應了對特斯拉Cybercab採用無稀土電機這一技術路線的看法。在9月8日晚間披露的投資者關係活動記錄中，金力永磁稱，高效能稀土永磁材料經過四十餘年持續的發展，目前正處於大規模擴大應用的階段。高效能釹鐵硼具有高磁能積、體積小、重量輕、高扭矩、控制精度優異等核心綜合優勢，有利於提高電機功率密度和執行效率，因此在新能源汽車高效能驅動電機、具身機器人及工業伺服電機等對小型化、輕量化和高扭矩密度要求嚴苛的高階領域，經過多年產業充分驗證，是不可替代的主流核心材料路線。（澎湃）

【SpaceX佔納指權重料獲提升 或迎來逾百億美元被動淨買盤】SpaceX股價近期持續窄幅波動，但隨著納斯達克100指數本月晚些時候進行季度再平衡，新一輪買盤可能很快到來。關鍵在於兩個因素：一是這一科技股權重較高的指數在編制方法中有一項較少受到關注的規則；二是埃隆·馬斯克這家衛星和太空探索公司的股東鎖定期分批到期，而這些鎖定期此前一直限制著可交易股份數量。納斯達克公司在確定成分股權重時，會根據公司市值進行計算，但採用已發行股份總數，或自由流通股數量的三倍，兩者取較低值。這意味著，如果一隻股票的自由流通股 —— 排除內部人士持股或仍處於鎖定期的股份 —— 比例較低，那麼其全部市值不會都被計入指數權重計算。

TSX Falls as Canada Retaliatory Tariffs Take Effect — The S&P/TSX Composite Index fell more than 0.5% to trade below 36,500 on Tuesday as Canada’s retaliatory tariffs on US goods took effect. The counter-tariffs cover $20 billion of US goods, with duties ranging from 15% to 50% on products including steel, furniture, clothing and electronics. The US tariffs implemented last month targeted sectors including wine, furniture, dairy products, cement, clothing, fishing rods and hockey equipment, covering $20 billion, or 5%, of Canadian exports to the US. Meanwhile, US President Trump said on Monday that Bombardier (-3%) would not be allowed to sell its planes in the US unless it started manufacturing in the country. Saputo shed about 0.5%, while Dorel lost more than 0.5%. Also weighing on the TSX, crude prices climbed after Saudi Arabia reported attacks that halted operations at several energy facilities. Fears of energy disruptions and energy-driven inflation increased rate hike expectations in Canada and the US, weighing on major banks.

【Tesla, 10+ automakers roll out price incentives; industry says demand-supply adjustment, not price war】Tesla China on Sept. 7 launched a limited-time in-stock incentive: orders taken and delivered by Sept. 30 qualify for a 5,000 yuan cash rebate on Model 3 variants and a 10,000 yuan rebate on Model Y variants; rebates can be combined with five-year 0% financing. Over the past two weeks more than ten brands across luxury, joint-venture, domestic and new-energy segments have offered time-limited purchase incentives — including in-stock cash rebates, insurance subsidies, zero-interest finance and enhanced trade-in offers. CPCA secretary-general Cui Dongshu said these measures are market actions to rebalance supply and demand during the key September sales window, describing them as pulse sales and inventory-clearance efforts rather than the start of a broad price war.

Ibovespa Jumps After Holiday — The Ibovespa jumped more than 1.5% to trade close to 188,000 on Tuesday following Monday’s holiday, posting broad-based gains. Petrobras rose more than 1.5% as oil prices surged following attacks on energy infrastructure in Saudi Arabia amid renewed hostilities in the Middle East. Vale gained about 2% as iron ore prices in China rose, driven by lower shipments and expectations of a seasonal recovery in Chinese demand. Meanwhile, a BTG/Nexus poll pointed to a technical tie between Flávio Bolsonaro and President Lula in a potential presidential runoff, although Flávio moved into a numerical lead of one percentage point. On Monday, a Quaest poll also showed the two tied at 41% in a second-round scenario. Flávio Bolsonaro is viewed by markets as more fiscally restrictive amid elevated yields and weak business activity. Major banks gained, with Itaú up more than 2% and Bradesco adding more than 1.5%. Utilities rose as well, with Axia gaining nearly 2%. WEG and Rede D’Or both rose about 2%.

Baltic Dry Index Bounces Back — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, rose about 0.3% to 3,584 points on Tuesday, following a 1.4% decrease in the prior session. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes including iron ore and coal, rebounded by 0.4% to 6,313 points; and the supramax index went up 0.7% to 1,693 points. On the other hand, the panamax index, which tracks vessels carrying around 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, was down for a third day, falling 0.7% to 2,414 points.

### 2026-09-08 21:35:47  #Trading Economics #Markets #United States #Stock Market #Importance 2

US Stocks Start Week Lower — US equities were lower on Tuesday as higher energy prices dented the outlook on margins and lifted risks of higher interest rates. The S&P 500 and Nasdaq 100 were 0.4% down, and the Dow fell nearly 1%. New strikes between the US and Iran prolonged expectations on suspended fuel exports from the Persian Gulf, while attacks from Houthi forces damaged Saudi energy infrastructure and dented their capacity. Borrowing costs for US companies were also lifted by hawkish signs from the Bank of Japan and likely selling of US Treasury securities by Japan to support the yen. Credit-sensitive hyperscalers were firmly lower, with Microsoft and Alphabet down 1%, while Amazon fell 1% ahead of its sterling bond offer. AI companies have increasingly borrowed in foreign currencies as dollar bond markets already face soaring AI-debt supply. In turn, Oracle gained 5% ahead of its earnings this week.

### 2026-09-08 21:22:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #Angola #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Angola inflation Rate Keeps Cooling — The annual inflation rate in Angola fell further to 8.78% in August 2026 from 9.33% in July, marking the lowest since April 2015 and extending the downward trend observed since mid-2024. The moderation in inflation has been attributed mainly to the relative stability of the kwanza and improvements in the domestic supply of essential goods. Price growth eased for several CPI categories, including food & non-alcoholic beverages (9.94% vs 10.40% in July); clothing & footwear (6.20% vs 6.30%); housing & utilities (8.24% vs 10.17%); miscellaneous goods & services (6.02% vs 6.38%) and education (19.33% vs 25.24%). Meanwhile, prices increased slightly faster for alcoholic beverages & tobacco (9.02% vs 8.61%) and transportation (3.67% vs 3.65%). On a monthly basis, the CPI rose by 0.59%, slowing from a 0.75% increase in the prior month.

### 2026-09-08 21:14:11  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Lumber Hits Nine-Month Low — Lumber futures fell below $555 per thousand board feet, reaching its lowest level in nine months, as retaliatory tariffs from Canada on US goods took effect, weighing down demand. Ottawa had announced counter-tariffs of 25% on US lumber and 50% on Plywood following US tariffs on several lumber-related goods. US forestry groups have long accused Canada of distorting the market, as 94% of Canadian forestland is publicly owned compared to 58% privately owned in the US, allowing Canadian lumber to be sold at a lower price. The fallout is hitting the forest sector of both countries, as selling lumber prices went up immediately after the news, pushing demand lower. Fall is usually a time for renovations, however, elevated prices weighs on the sector. Meanwhile, US housing remains subdued, with lumber-intensive single-family construction spending falling 3.2% in July, while elevated mortgage rates continue to weigh on homeownership.

---

### 2026-09-08 21:14:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Used Car Prices MoM #Importance 1

US Used-Car Prices Fall for Second Month — The Manheim Used Vehicle Value Index, a widely followed gauge of wholesale used-vehicle prices in the US, fell 0.9% month-on-month in August 2026, marking a second consecutive monthly decline. On a yearly basis, price growth slowed to 0.4%, the weakest pace since December 2025, as resilience in EV and compact-car prices was offset by continued declines in pickups and SUVs. Cox Automotive Chief Economist Jeremy Robb said depreciation was stronger in July and early August before easing toward the end of the month. While younger vehicle cohorts saw greater depreciation, older and more affordable cars held up better, with nine- and 10-year-old vehicles remaining about six percentage points above their long-term averages, underscoring steady demand for affordable used cars. Used retail sales continued to strengthen through August, while retail days’ supply fell to 44 days from 46 at the end of July. Stronger demand and tighter supply continued to support dealer purchases from Manheim.

### 2026-09-08 21:09:30  #市場

【全國豬肉價格連續4週上漲 豬肉價格上漲原因找到了】近期，在北京批發市場走訪瞭解到，受需求增長拉動，豬肉價格溫和回升。資料顯示，9月8日，北京新發地市場白條豬批發平均價為每公斤14元，比8月1日上漲12%。據介紹，豬肉價格上漲，一方面是因為天氣逐漸轉涼、肉類消費增加，另外，能繁母豬存欄量的下降也帶來了豬價上漲的預期。北京新發地市場分析師劉通判斷，從總體上來看，回升的態勢比較溫和，由於毛豬的存欄量仍然比較充足，所以後期肉價的漲勢也不會太大。從全國來看，全國豬肉價格和生豬價格也是呈現回升走勢。據農業農村部監測，9月份第1周全國生豬價格為每公斤11.59元，環比上漲0.3%，豬肉價格為每公斤21.23元，環比上漲0.2%，生豬價格和豬肉價格均是連續4週上漲。（央視財經）
